File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/allInvoicesAreInvalidMapping.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE allInvoicesAreInvalidMapping
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN('JSON');
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot.JSON NAME 'Data';
		DECLARE output REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			
		SET output.summary.proccessed = '0';
		SET output.summary.successful = '0';
		SET output.summary.failed = '0';
		SET output.status = 'failed';	
		CREATE FIELD output.results IDENTITY (JSON.Array) results;
		SET output.results.Item[1].reference VALUE = NULL;
		SET output.results.Item[1].status VALUE = NULL;
		SET output.results.Item[1].message VALUE = NULL;
		
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_ID";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."FIP_NUMBER";
				
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/asv/PrepareHTTPRequest.esql

BROKER SCHEMA asv

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareHTTPRequestMapping
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = Environment.Variables.Policy.RequestURL_invoice ;
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'POST';
		
        CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data TYPE JSON.Array;
        DECLARE outRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
        
		DECLARE invoiceCount INTEGER CARDINALITY(InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[]);
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		-- Loop through all invoices
		WHILE I <= invoiceCount DO
			
			DECLARE refInvoice REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[I];
			
	        -- Create the I item in the array
	        CREATE FIELD outRef.Item[I];
	        DECLARE refItem REFERENCE TO outRef.Item[I];
	        
	        -- Create the "process-control" object
	        CREATE FIELD refItem."process-control"."specification-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
	        DECLARE refSpecIdentifier REFERENCE TO refItem."process-control"."specification-identifier";
	        SET refSpecIdentifier.Item[1]."content" = 'ASV_INTERVENTION_CUSTOMER_BILLING';

	        -- Invoice type code and currency
	        IF refInvoice.document_type = 1
	        THEN
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = 380;
	        ELSEIF refInvoice.document_type = 2
	        THEN
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = 381;
	        ELSE
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = refInvoice.document_type;
	        END IF;
	        	
	        SET refItem."invoice-currency-code".content = 'CHF';
	        SET refItem."precending-invoice-reference".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.invoice_id, '');
	        
	        -- Create the "invoice-number" array
	        CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-number" TYPE JSON.Array;
	        DECLARE refInvNum REFERENCE TO refItem."invoice-number";
	        SET refInvNum.Item[1]."content" = CAST(refInvoice.id AS CHARACTER);
	        SET refInvNum.Item[1]."scheme-identifier" = 'INVOICE_ID';
	        SET refInvNum.Item[2]."content" = CAST(refInvoice.patient.fip_number AS CHARACTER);
	        SET refInvNum.Item[2]."scheme-identifier" = 'PATIENT_FIP_NUMBER';
	
			-- Invoice issue date
	        SET refItem."invoice-issue-date".content = refInvoice.date;
	
	        -- Seller details
	        SET refItem.seller."seller-name".content = 'ASV';
	        SET refItem.seller."seller-postal-address"."country-code".content = 'CH';
	
	        -- VAT breakdown
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-taxable-amount".content = 0;
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-tax-amount".content = 0;
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-code".content = 'E';
	
			-- Document totals
			CREATE FIELD refItem."document-totals";
			
			-- Amount due for payment
			SET refItem."document-totals"."amount-due-for-payment".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Sum of invoice line net amount
			SET refItem."document-totals"."sum-of-invoice-line-net-amount".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Invoice total amount without VAT
			SET refItem."document-totals"."invoice-total-amount-without-vat".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Invoice total amount with VAT
			SET refItem."document-totals"."invoice-total-amount-with-vat".content = refInvoice.total_price;
	
			-- Create the "invoice-note" array
			CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-note" TYPE JSON.Array;
			DECLARE refInvNote REFERENCE TO refItem."invoice-note";
			CREATE FIELD refInvNote.Item[1];
		
			SET refInvNote.Item[1]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_TYPE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[1]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.type, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[2]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_START_TIME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[2]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.start_time, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[3]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_END_TIME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[3]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.end_time, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[4]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[4]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[5]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_STREET';
			SET refInvNote.Item[5]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_street, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[6]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_STREET_NUMBER';
			SET refInvNote.Item[6]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_street_number, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[7]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_COMPLEMENT';
			SET refInvNote.Item[7]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_complement, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[8]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_POSTAL_CODE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[8]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_postal_code, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[9]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_CITY';
			SET refInvNote.Item[9]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_city, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[10]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_COUNTRY_CODE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[10]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_country_code, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[11]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[11]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[12]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_STREET';
			SET refInvNote.Item[12]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_street, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[13]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_STREET_NUMBER';
			SET refInvNote.Item[13]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_street_number, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[14]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_COMPLEMENT';
			SET refInvNote.Item[14]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_complement, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[15]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_POSTAL_CODE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[15]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_postal_code, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[16]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_CITY';
			SET refInvNote.Item[16]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_city, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[17]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_DESTINATION_COUNTRY_CODE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[17]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.destination_country_code, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[18]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_INSURANCE_POLICY_NUMBER';
			SET refInvNote.Item[18]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.insurance_policy_number, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[19]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_CATEGORY_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[19]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.category_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[20]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_CATEGORY_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[20]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.category_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[21]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_GENDER_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[21]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.gender_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[22]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_GENDER_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[22]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.gender_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[23]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LASTNAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[23]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.lastname, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[24]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_FIRSTNAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[24]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.firstname, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[25]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_BIRTHDATE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[25]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.birthdate, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[26]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_STREET';
			SET refInvNote.Item[26]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.street, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[27]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_STREET_NUMBER';
			SET refInvNote.Item[27]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.street_number, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[28]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_COMPLEMENT';
			SET refInvNote.Item[28]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.complement, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[29]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_PO_BOX';
			SET refInvNote.Item[29]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.po_box, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[30]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_POSTAL_CODE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[30]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.postal_code, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[31]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_CITY';
			SET refInvNote.Item[31]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.city, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[32]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_CANTON_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[32]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.canton_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[33]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_COUNTRY_CODE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[33]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.country_code, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[34]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_COUNTRY_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[34]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.country_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[35]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_INSURANCE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[35]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.insurance_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[36]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[36]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[37]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_KILOMETERS';
			SET refInvNote.Item[37]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.kilometers, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[38]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_NACA_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[38]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.naca_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[39]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ORGANIZATION_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[39]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.organization_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[40]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ORGANIZATION_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[40]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.organization_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[41]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_BASE_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[41]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.base_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[42]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_BASE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[42]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.base_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[43]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PARTNER_TYPE_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[43]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.type_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[44]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PARTNER_TYPE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[44]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.type_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[45]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PARTNER_GENDER_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[45]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.gender_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[46]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PARTNER_GENDER';
			SET refInvNote.Item[46]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.gender_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[47]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ALARMED_BY_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[47]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.alarmed_by_id, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[48]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ALARMED_BY_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[48]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.alarmed_by_name, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[49]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'REFERENCE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[49]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.reference, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[50]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'COMMENTS';
			SET refInvNote.Item[50]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.comments, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[51]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LEGAL_REPRESENTATIVE_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[51]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.legal_representative_id, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[52]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LEGAL_REPRESENTATIVE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[52]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.legal_representative_name, '');
			-- Buyer details
			IF refInvoice.debtor.type_id = 2
			THEN
				SET refItem.buyer."buyer-trading-name".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.name, '');
			END IF;
			
			-- Buyer name (lastname|firstname if both present)
			IF refInvoice.debtor.type_id = 1
			THEN
				SET refItem.buyer."buyer-name".content =
				CASE 
			        WHEN refInvoice.debtor.lastname IS NOT NULL AND refInvoice.debtor.firstname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			        	COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.lastname, '') || '|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.firstname, '')
			        WHEN refInvoice.debtor.lastname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			        	COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.lastname, '') || '|'
			   		WHEN refInvoice.debtor.firstname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			         	'|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.firstname, '')
			    	ELSE ''
			  	END;
			END IF;
			-- Buyer postal address
			-- Address line 1 (street|street_number if both present)
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-1".content =
				CASE 
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street IS NOT NULL AND refInvoice.debtor.street_number IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street, '') || '|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street_number, '')
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street, '')
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street_number IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street_number, '')
			  		ELSE ''
			  	END;
			
			-- Address line 2
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-2".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.complement, '');	
			-- Address line 3
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-3".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.po_box, '');		
			-- Post code
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."post-code".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.postal_code, '');		
			-- City
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address".city.content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.city, '');
	
			-- Country code
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."country-code".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.country_code, '');	
					    
			 -- Create the "invoice-line" array
			CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-line" TYPE JSON.Array;		
			DECLARE articleRef REFERENCE TO refInvoice.articles.Item[1];
			DECLARE i INTEGER 1;
			
			-- Loop through the articles in the input JSON
			WHILE LASTMOVE(articleRef) DO
				SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-description".content = COALESCE(articleRef.line_2, '');
			   
			    -- Add "item-name" object inside "item-information"
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-name".content = COALESCE(articleRef.line_1, '');
			
			    -- Add "item-sellers-identifier" array inside "item-information"
			    CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier".Item[1]."content" = COALESCE(articleRef.code, '');
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier".Item[1]."scheme-identifier" = 'ARTICLE_CODE';
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-quantity" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-quantity".content = COALESCE(CAST(CAST(articleRef.quantity AS DECIMAL) AS CHARACTER FORMAT '##0.00'), '');
			
			    -- Add "price-details" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."price-details"."item-net-price".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.unit_price AS CHARACTER), '');
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-line-net-amount" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-line-net-amount".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.price AS CHARACTER), '');
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-quantity-unit-of-measure-code" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-quantity-unit-of-measure-code".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.unit_name AS CHARACTER), '');
			    
			    MOVE articleRef NEXTSIBLING;
			    SET i = i + 1;
			END WHILE;
			
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/emptyOrMissingFileError.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE emptyOrMissingFileError
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		IF InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.NoMatchReason = 'NoFile' THEN			
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'WARN';

			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.meta.submissionId = COALESCE(NULLIF(Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id", ''),COALESCE(NULLIF(Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id", ''),COALESCE(NULLIF(Environment.Variables.trackID, ''), UUIDASCHAR)));
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.meta.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyy-MM-dd''T''HH:mm:ss');
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.status = 'NO_FILE';
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.receivedCount = 0;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.message = '';

			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 200;

			RETURN TRUE;
		END IF;

		IF Environment.invalidFile = TRUE THEN			
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERR';

			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 422;

			DECLARE output REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = '422';
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = 'Unprocessable Entity';
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = 'Invalid file format or content. The payload could not be parsed.';
			
			RETURN TRUE;
		END IF;
	RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

components:
  schemas:
    ServiceError:
      title: ServiceError
      type: object
      description: Object returned when errors occurred
      properties:
        httpCode:
          type: string
        httpMessage:
          type: string
        moreInformation:
          type: string
      required:
        - httpCode
        - httpMessage
        - moreInformation

    IngestionResponse:
      title: IngestionResponse
      type: object
      description: Detailed response for invoice ingestion, including per-invoice results.
      properties:
        status:
          type: string
          description: Overall status of the ingestion process.
          enum: [success, partial_success, failure]
          example: partial_success
        summary:
          type: object
          description: Summary of the ingestion results.
          properties:
            processed:
              type: integer
              example: 5
            successful:
              type: integer
              example: 3
            failed:
              type: integer
              example: 2
          required:
            - processed
            - successful
            - failed
        results:
          type: array
          description: List of individual invoice processing results.
          items:
            type: object
            properties:
              reference:
                type: string
                description: FIP number associated with the invoice.
                example: "123"
              status:
                type: string
                description: Status of the invoice processing.
                enum: [success, failure]
                example: success
              message:
                type: string
                description: Message describing the result.
                example: Invoice processed successfully.
            required:
              - reference
              - status
              - message
      required:
        - status
        - summary
        - results

    PurgeResponse:
      title: PurgeResponse
      type: object
      description: Response returned when the Purge is successfully triggered.
      properties:
        httpCode:
          type: integer
          example: 202
        httpMessage:
          type: string
          example: "Accepted"
        moreInformation:
          type: string
          example: "ASV File Purge triggered properly"
      required:
        - httpCode
        - httpMessage
        - moreInformation
        
    HealthType:
      type: object
      properties:
        status:
          type: string
          description: status of the health check
          enum: [pass, error]
        api:
          type: string
          description: api/app name of the component
        timestamp:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        checks:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/Check'
    Check:
      type: object
      description: 'describe a component check (MQ, DataBase, BackEnd etc..)'
      properties:
        component:
          type: string
          description: 'name of the tested component (MQ, Baloise, Toni, etc..)'
        status:
          type: string
          description: status of the tested component
          enum: [pass, error, warn]
        details:
          type: string
          description: details

  examples:
    EmptyProcessing:
      summary: No invoices processed
      value:
        httpCode: '500'
        httpMessage: 'Internal server error'
        moreInformation: 'Error reading file. File is missing, file is empty or file is bad formated(JSON)'
    TechnicalIssueOnTheFlow:
      summary: Either ACE app / SAP is having tehcnical issue
      value:
        httpCode: '500'
        httpMessage: 'Internal server error'
        moreInformation: 'Error calling SAP. Invoices moved to error file'
    FailedProcessing:
      summary: All invoices failed
      value:
        status: failure
        summary:
          processed: 2
          successful: 0
          failed: 2
        results: 
          - reference: '123'
            status: failure
            message: Invoice not processed.
          - reference: '456'
            status: failure
            message: Invoice not processed.
    SuccessfulProcessing:
      summary: All invoices processed successfully
      value:
        status: success
        summary:
          processed: 2
          successful: 2
          failed: 0
        results: 
          - reference: '123'
            status: success
            message: Invoice processed successfully.
          - reference: '456'
            status: success
            message: Invoice processed successfully.
    PartialFailureProcessing:
      summary: Some invoices failed
      value:
        status: partial_success
        summary:
          processed: 5
          successful: 3
          failed: 2
        results:
        - reference: '123'
          status: success
          message: Invoice processed successfully.
        - reference: '456'
          status: success
          message: Invoice processed successfully.
        - reference: '789'
          status: failure
          message: 'Validation error: missing tax code.'
        - reference: '101112'
          status: success
          message: Invoice processed successfully.
        - reference: '131415'
          status: failure
          message: Internal server error during BAPI execution.

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/gen/FileResponseOK.esql

BROKER SCHEMA gen

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE FileResponseOK
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET Environment.Variables.FILEResponse = 'OK';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE FileResponseERROR
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET Environment.Variables.FILEResponse = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.Error = TRUE;

		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		SET variablesRef.errorMessage = messageText;
		SET variablesRef.errorCode = CAST(messageCode AS CHARACTER);

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE FileReadNoMatch
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET Environment.Variables.StopFlux = TRUE;
		IF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.Error = TRUE;
		END IF;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/gen/purgeFile.esql

BROKER SCHEMA gen
DECLARE DATE_FILE_FORMAT EXTERNAL CHARACTER 'yyyyMMdd';
DECLARE DATE_SAP_FORMAT EXTERNAL CHARACTER 'yyyyMMdd';
--DECLARE TIME_SAP_FORMAT EXTERNAL CHARACTER 'hh:mm:ss';

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE ScanDirCheckDate
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE test CHARACTER SUBSTRING(InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name FROM 0 FOR LENGTH(DATE_FILE_FORMAT)+1);
		DECLARE testInteger BOOLEAN;
		CALL testFormatInteger(SUBSTRING(InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name FROM 0 FOR LENGTH(DATE_FILE_FORMAT)+1 ),testInteger);
		IF testInteger THEN
			DECLARE DateAsCharFile CHARACTER SUBSTRING(InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name FROM 0 FOR LENGTH(DATE_FILE_FORMAT)+1);
			DECLARE DateAsDateFile DATE CAST(DateAsCharFile AS DATE FORMAT DATE_FILE_FORMAT);
			DECLARE CURRENTDAY DATE CAST(CURRENT_DATE AS DATE FORMAT DATE_FILE_FORMAT);
			DECLARE inDays INTEGER CAST((CURRENTDAY-DateAsDateFile)DAY AS INTEGER);		
			IF inDays < 90 THEN	
				DECLARE IDX INTEGER CARDINALITY(Environment.Variables.Files.File[]);
				SET Environment.Variables.Files.File[IDX+1].Name = InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name;
				SET Environment.Variables.Files.File[IDX+1].Directory = InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.TransferredFile.ServerDirectory;
				SET Environment.Variables.Files.File[IDX+1].BLOB = InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB;
			ELSE 
				SET Environment.Variables.filePurgedCount = Environment.Variables.filePurgedCount + 1 ;
				SET Environment.Variables.filePurgedName = Environment.Variables.filePurgedName || InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.TransferredFile.ServerDirectory || '/'||InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name || '|';
				DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers";
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers".filePurged = InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name;
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers".directoryPurge = InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.TransferredFile.ServerDirectory;
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out' DELETE NONE;
			END IF;
		ELSE
			DECLARE IDX INTEGER CARDINALITY(Environment.Variables.Files.File[]);
			SET Environment.Variables.Files.File[IDX+1].Name = InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name;
			SET Environment.Variables.Files.File[IDX+1].Directory = InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.TransferredFile.ServerDirectory;
			SET Environment.Variables.Files.File[IDX+1].BLOB = InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB;
		END IF;
		RETURN FALSE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Orchestrate
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN		
		CREATE FIELD Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		DECLARE RefSftpEndPointsPolicy REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		call Shlib_PoliciesReader.getAllPolicyProperties('finance-integration-policies-endpoints','SftpEndPointsPolicy',RefSftpEndPointsPolicy);
		if EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.error[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('''{finance-integration-policies-endpoints''}:SftpEndPointsPolicy not found'); 
		elseif not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_purge_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY not found'); 
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_purge_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY not found');
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_purge_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY not found');
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_INPUT_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_purge_INVOICE_INPUT_DIRECTORY not found');
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY not found');
		end if;
		
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 202;
		
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data."httpCode" = 202;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data."httpMessage" = 'Accepted';
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data."moreInformation" = 'File Purge triggered properly';
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1' DELETE NONE;
		
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP22160';--SFTP
		WHILE NOT(variablesRef.StopFlux) DO 
			DELETE FIELD OutputLocalEnvironment;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY;
			SET variablesRef.Info.DirectoryRead = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY;
			DELETE FIELD variablesRef.TCSLog."business-identifiers" ;
			SET variablesRef.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."DirectoryRead" = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out' DELETE NONE;
		END WHILE;
		SET variablesRef.StopFlux = FALSE;
		WHILE NOT(variablesRef.StopFlux) DO 
			DELETE FIELD OutputLocalEnvironment;
			
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY;
			SET variablesRef.Info.DirectoryRead = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY;
			DELETE FIELD variablesRef.TCSLog."business-identifiers" ;
			SET variablesRef.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."DirectoryRead" = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out' DELETE NONE;
		END WHILE;
		SET variablesRef.StopFlux = FALSE;
		WHILE NOT(variablesRef.StopFlux) DO 
			DELETE FIELD OutputLocalEnvironment;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY;
			SET variablesRef.Info.DirectoryRead = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY;
			DELETE FIELD variablesRef.TCSLog."business-identifiers" ;
			SET variablesRef.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."DirectoryRead" = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out' DELETE NONE;
		END WHILE;
		DELETE FIELD OutputLocalEnvironment;
		FOR File AS variablesRef.Files.File[] DO
			DELETE FIELD variablesRef.Info ;
			SET variablesRef.Info."FileDirectory" = File.Directory;
			SET variablesRef.Info."FileName" = File.Name;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Name =  File.Name ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = File.Directory ;
			SET OutputRoot.BLOB.BLOB = File.BLOB ;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2' DELETE NONE;
		END FOR;
		DELETE FIELD OutputRoot ;
		DELETE FIELD variablesRef.Info ;
		SET variablesRef.Info.filePurgedCount = variablesRef.filePurgedCount ; 
		SET variablesRef.Info.filePurgedName = variablesRef.filePurgedName; 
		
		IF variablesRef.Error THEN
			SET variablesRef.endLog = 'INFO: ERROR: PROCESS PURGE ERROR';
		ELSE
			SET variablesRef.endLog = 'INFO: END Process Purge';
		END IF;
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out3' DELETE NONE;
		RETURN FALSE;
	END;
END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE FileReadNoMatchPurge
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET Environment.Variables.StopFlux = TRUE;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'ACE';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE HandlingError
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET variablesRef.StopFlux = TRUE;
		DECLARE RefSftpEndPointsPolicy REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		
		DELETE FIELD variablesRef.TCSLog.context."error-message" ;
		DELETE FIELD variablesRef.TCSLog.context."error-code" ;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Name = variablesRef.FileName;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_purge_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		SET OutputRoot.BLOB.BLOB = variablesRef.XMLNSC.Message ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 500;
	
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;
/*
* A procedure that will get the message and number error for logging

*
*/
CREATE PROCEDURE getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(IN InputTree REFERENCE,OUT messageNumber INTEGER, OUT messageText CHARACTER)
BEGIN
	-- Create a reference to the first child of the exception list
	DECLARE ptrException REFERENCE TO InputTree.*[1];
	-- keep looping while the moves to the child of exception list work
	WHILE lastmove(ptrException) DO
		-- store the current values for the error number and text
		IF ptrException.Number IS NOT NULL THEN
			SET messageNumber = ptrException.Number;
			SET messageText = ptrException.Text||': ';

			DECLARE i INTEGER 0;
			WHILE ptrException.Insert[i+1].Text IS NOT NULL DO
				SET messageText = messageText || ptrException.Insert[i+1].Text;
				SET i = i+1;
			END WHILE;
		END IF;
		-- now move to the last child which should be the next exceptionlist
		MOVE ptrException LASTCHILD;
	END WHILE;
END;
/*
* Module allows to create UUID and set context for log
*
* Parameters:
*
* RETURNS: NOTHING
*
*/
CREATE COMPUTE MODULE SetTCSLog
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot=InputRoot;
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;
		SET Environment.Variables.XCorrelationID = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05737';--APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';--ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.filePurgedCount = 0;
		SET Environment.Variables.filePurgedName = '|';
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1' DELETE NONE;
		SET Environment.Variables.StopFlux = FALSE;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;
/*
* Module allows to set context for log
*
* Parameters:
*
* RETURNS: NOTHING
*
*/
CREATE COMPUTE MODULE EndTCSLog
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."Content-Type" = 'text/json';
    	SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Correlation-ID" =  Environment.Variables.XCorrelationID ;
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller";
		SET OutputRoot.JSON = InputRoot.JSON ;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05737';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

/*
* A procedure that will test if the CHARACTER IS an INTEGER

*
*/
CREATE PROCEDURE testFormatInteger(IN chartotest CHARACTER,OUT integerOrNot BOOLEAN)
BEGIN
	SET integerOrNot = TRUE;
	DECLARE charposition INTEGER 1;
	--DECLARE charposition2 CHARACTER chartotest[0];
	
	DECLARE charvalid CHARACTER '0123456789';
	WHILE charposition <= LENGTH(chartotest) DO	
		IF NOT(CONTAINS(charvalid,SUBSTRING(chartotest FROM charposition FOR 1))) THEN
			SET integerOrNot = FALSE;
		END IF;
		SET charposition = charposition +1;
	END WHILE;
END;

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/PrepareAndProcessFile.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareProcessFile
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		-- extract policy values
		DECLARE "ace-host-finance" CHARACTER Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy',  'ace-host-finance');
		
		SET Environment.Variables.Policy.RequestURL_invoice = "ace-host-finance" || Shlib_PoliciesReader.getPolicyProperty('{finance-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndPointsPolicy', 'path-finance-accounting-integration-invoice');
		
		CREATE FIELD Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		DECLARE RefSftpEndPointsPolicy REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		call Shlib_PoliciesReader.getAllPolicyProperties('finance-integration-policies-endpoints','SftpEndPointsPolicy',RefSftpEndPointsPolicy);
		if EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.error[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('''{finance-integration-policies-endpoints''}:SftpEndPointsPolicy not found'); 
		elseif not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_injection_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY not found'); 
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_injection_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY not found');
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_injection_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY not found');
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_INPUT_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_injection_INVOICE_INPUT_DIRECTORY not found');
		elseif	  not EXISTS(RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY[]) then
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('property post_injection_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY not found');
		end if;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_INPUT_DIRECTORY;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareSaveFile
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		DECLARE currentTimestamp CHARACTER CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss');
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Name =  currentTimestamp || '_' || InputLocalEnvironment.File.Read.Name;
		DECLARE RefSftpEndPointsPolicy REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_SAVE_DIRECTORY;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE post_injection_tcs_invoice_split_validation
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		-- ERROR HANDLE will execute the code inside in case of any error
		DECLARE EXIT HANDLER FOR SQLSTATE VALUE ''
        BEGIN
        	SET Environment.invalidFile = TRUE;
        	PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out3' DELETE NONE;
            RETURN FALSE ;
        END;
		
		
		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.Item[]) = FALSE THEN
			SET Environment.invalidFile = TRUE;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out3' DELETE NONE;
			RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
		
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		DECLARE inputRef REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices;
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE invoiceCount INTEGER;
		DECLARE validationErrorCount INTEGER 0;
		
		-- Get the number of invoices
		SET invoiceCount = CARDINALITY(inputRef.[]);
		DECLARE RefSftpEndPointsPolicy REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		-- Loop through all invoices
		WHILE I <= invoiceCount DO

			DECLARE jsonDoc REFERENCE TO inputRef.[I];
			DECLARE articleList REFERENCE TO jsonDoc.articles;
			DECLARE debtorRef REFERENCE TO jsonDoc.debtor;
			DECLARE patientRef REFERENCE TO jsonDoc.patient;
			DECLARE interventionRef REFERENCE TO jsonDoc.intervention;
			DECLARE validationFailed BOOLEAN FALSE;
			
			Validation:	LOOP 
				IF FIELDNAME(jsonDoc.id) IS NULL OR
				   FIELDNAME(jsonDoc.date) IS NULL OR
				   FIELDNAME(jsonDoc.total_price) IS NULL OR
				   FIELDNAME(jsonDoc.intervention) IS NULL OR
				   FIELDNAME(jsonDoc.articles) IS NULL OR
				   FIELDNAME(jsonDoc.patient) IS NULL THEN
					SET validationFailed = TRUE;
					LEAVE Validation;
				END IF;

				IF COALESCE(jsonDoc.document_type, '') = '2' AND COALESCE(jsonDoc.invoice_id, '') = ''
				THEN
					SET validationFailed = TRUE;
					LEAVE Validation;
				END IF;

				IF FIELDNAME(interventionRef) IS NOT NULL THEN
					IF FIELDNAME(interventionRef.id) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(interventionRef.type) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(interventionRef.organization_id) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(interventionRef.organization_name) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(interventionRef.base_id) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(interventionRef.base_name) IS NULL THEN
						SET validationFailed = TRUE;
						LEAVE Validation;
					END IF;
				END IF;
				
				IF FIELDNAME(patientRef) IS NOT NULL THEN
					IF FIELDNAME(patientRef.fip_number) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(patientRef.category_id) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(patientRef.category_name) IS NULL THEN
						SET validationFailed = TRUE;
						LEAVE Validation;
					END IF;
				END IF;
				
				IF FIELDNAME(debtorRef) IS NOT NULL THEN
					IF FIELDNAME(debtorRef.type_id) IS NULL OR
					   FIELDNAME(debtorRef.type_name) IS NULL THEN
						SET validationFailed = TRUE;
						LEAVE Validation;
					END IF;
				END IF;
								
				IF FIELDNAME(articleList) IS NOT NULL THEN
					MOVE articleList FIRSTCHILD;
					WHILE LASTMOVE(articleList) DO
						IF FIELDNAME(articleList.line_1) IS NULL THEN
							SET validationFailed = TRUE;
							LEAVE Validation;
						END IF;
						MOVE articleList NEXTSIBLING;
					END WHILE;
				END IF;
				LEAVE Validation;
			END LOOP Validation;
		
			IF validationFailed THEN
				DELETE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[I - validationErrorCount];
				SET validationErrorCount = validationErrorCount + 1;
				IF EXISTS(Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson[]) = FALSE THEN
					CREATE LASTCHILD OF Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson DOMAIN('JSON');
					CREATE FIELD Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson.JSON.Data.(JSON.Array)invoices;
				END IF;
				SET Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson.JSON.Data.invoices.Item[validationErrorCount] = jsonDoc;
			ELSE
				--ADD business-id
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_ID" = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_ID", '') 
																								|| '|' || CAST(jsonDoc.id AS CHARACTER);
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."FIP_NUMBER" = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."FIP_NUMBER", '') 
																								|| '|' || CAST(patientRef.fip_number AS CHARACTER);
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_REFERENCE" = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_REFERENCE", '') 
																								|| '|' || CAST(jsonDoc.invoice_id AS CHARACTER);
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."DOCUMENT_TYPE" = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."DOCUMENT_TYPE", '') 
																								|| '|' || CAST(jsonDoc.document_type AS CHARACTER);
			END IF;
			
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;		
		
		-- Checks if all invoices are invalid Send valid messages to routing
		IF EXISTS(OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.Item[1].id[]) = false THEN
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out4' DELETE NONE;
		ELSE 
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1' DELETE NONE;
		END IF;
		
		-- If SAP call fails move all invoices fip to array
		IF Environment.sapCallFail = 'true' THEN
			FOR outInvoice AS OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.Item[] DO
				CREATE LASTCHILD OF Environment.failedInvoices NAME 'fipNumber' VALUE outInvoice.patient.fip_number;
			END FOR;
		END IF;
		
		-- Deletes unsuccessful invoices from SAP response
		DECLARE sapRejectedInvoicesCount INTEGER CARDINALITY(Environment.failedInvoices.fipNumber[]);
		DECLARE outInvoicesCount INTEGER CARDINALITY(OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.Item[]);
		DECLARE i INTEGER 1;
		IF sapRejectedInvoicesCount > 0 THEN	
			WHILE i <= outInvoicesCount DO
				DECLARE outInvoice REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.Item[i];
			 	FOR fipNumber AS Environment.failedInvoices.fipNumber[] DO
					IF outInvoice.patient.fip_number = fipNumber THEN
						IF EXISTS(Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson[]) = FALSE THEN
							CREATE LASTCHILD OF Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson DOMAIN('JSON');
							CREATE FIELD Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson.JSON.Data.(JSON.Array)invoices;
						END IF;
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson.JSON.Data.invoices FROM outInvoice;
						DELETE FIELD outInvoice;
						SET i = i - 1;
						SET outInvoicesCount = outInvoicesCount - 1;
					END IF;
				END FOR;
				SET i = i + 1;
			END WHILE;
		END IF;

		
		--Send Report Finish validation
		SET Environment.Variables.ValidInvoicesCount = invoiceCount - validationErrorCount - sapRejectedInvoicesCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.InvalidInvoicesCount = validationErrorCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.SapRejectedInvoicesCount = sapRejectedInvoicesCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.InvoiceCount = invoiceCount;
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out' DELETE NONE;
		
		--Write files if Validation error
		IF validationErrorCount > 0 OR sapRejectedInvoicesCount > 0 THEN
			SET OutputRoot = NULL;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN('JSON');
			CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices IDENTITY (JSON.Array)invoices;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices = Environment.Variables.ValidationFailedJson.JSON.Data.invoices;
			DECLARE currentTimestamp CHARACTER CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss');
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = 400;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Name = currentTimestamp || '_validation_failed_invoices.json';
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_ERROR_DIRECTORY;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2';
		END IF;
		RETURN FALSE;
	END;
END MODULE;

/*
* Module allows to create HTTP response 
*
* Parameters:
*
* RETURNS: NOTHING
*
*/
CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareOutFiles
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		--Write files if Validation error
		DECLARE ValidInvoicesCount INTEGER Environment.Variables.ValidInvoicesCount;
		DECLARE InvalidInvoicesCount INTEGER Environment.Variables.InvalidInvoicesCount;
		DECLARE RejectedInvoicesCount INTEGER Environment.Variables.SapRejectedInvoicesCount;
		DECLARE TotalInvoicesCount INTEGER Environment.Variables.InvoiceCount;
		DECLARE RefSftpEndPointsPolicy REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Policy."finance-integration-policies-endpoints".SftpEndPointsPolicy;
		
		IF ValidInvoicesCount > 0 THEN
			DECLARE currentTimestamp CHARACTER CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss');
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Name = currentTimestamp ||'_invoices.json';
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Directory = RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY ;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.File.Remote.ServerDirectory =RefSftpEndPointsPolicy.post_injection_INVOICE_DONE_DIRECTORY;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out';
		END IF;
		
		--Generate Report
		DECLARE EventMessage, ingestionStatus CHARACTER;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers" = NULL;
		IF TotalInvoicesCount = ValidInvoicesCount THEN
			SET EventMessage = 'SUP: One File processed successully';
			SET ingestionStatus = 'Success';	
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		ELSEIF TotalInvoicesCount = InvalidInvoicesCount + RejectedInvoicesCount THEN
			SET EventMessage = 'SUP: One File failed to be processed entirely';
			SET ingestionStatus = 'Failure';	
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERR';
		ELSE 
			SET EventMessage = 'SUP: One File processed with errors';
			SET ingestionStatus = 'Partial Success';
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'WARN';
		END IF;	
			
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INGESTION_STATUS" = ingestionStatus;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."TOTAL_PROCESSED_INVOICES" = TotalInvoicesCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."TOTAL_SUCCESFULLY_PROCESSED_INVOICES" = ValidInvoicesCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."TOTAL_WRONG_INPUT_FORMAT_ERRORS" = InvalidInvoicesCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."TOTAL_REJECTION_ERRORS" = RejectedInvoicesCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.EventMessage = EventMessage;
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2';
		RETURN FALSE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/sapCallingError.esql


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE sapCallingError
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE enc INTEGER COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.Encoding, 546);
		DECLARE charSet INTEGER COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		DECLARE code, message, details CHARACTER;

		SET Environment.sapCallFail = 'true';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERR';	

		CASE
			WHEN CARDINALITY(InputExceptionList.*[]) > 0
			THEN
				DECLARE number_out INTEGER;
				DECLARE text_out CHARACTER;
				DECLARE stacktrace_out CHARACTER;

				SET code = '500';

				CALL GetException(InputExceptionList, number_out, text_out, stacktrace_out);

				IF COALESCE(text_out, '') <> ''
				THEN
					SET message = 'Invoices moved to error file. ' || COALESCE(CAST(number_out AS CHARACTER), '0000') || ' - ' || text_out;
				ELSE
					SET message = 'Invoices moved to error file.';
				END IF;

				IF COALESCE(stacktrace_out, '') <> ''
				THEN
					SET details = stacktrace_out;
				ELSE
					SET details = 'Internal Server Error.';
				END IF;
			WHEN CARDINALITY(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader[]) > 0
			THEN
				DECLARE responseAsBlob BLOB;
				IF EXISTS(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB[])
				THEN
					SET responseAsBlob  = InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB;
				END IF;

				SET code = CAST(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code" AS CHARACTER);
				SET message = 'Invoices moved to error file.';
				SET details = CAST(responseAsBlob AS CHARACTER CCSID charSet Encoding enc);
			ELSE
				SET code = '500';
				SET message = 'Invoices moved to error file.';
				SET details = 'Internal Server Error.';
		END CASE;

		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = code;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = message;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = details;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = COALESCE(CAST(code AS INTEGER DEFAULT NULL), 500);

		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_ID";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."FIP_NUMBER";
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE FUNCTION GetException(IN InputTree reference, OUT number_out integer, OUT text_out char, OUT stacktrace_out char)
	BEGIN
		Declare stacktrace CHARACTER '';
		DECLARE start, tempo REFERENCE TO InputExceptionList.*[1];

		WHILE start.Number IS NOT NULL
		DO
			SET stacktrace = CAST(start.Number as CHARACTER) || ':' || start.Text || ':' || COALESCE(start.Label, '') || ' '|| stacktrace;
			SET number_out = start.Number;
			MOVE tempo TO start;
			MOVE start LASTCHILD;
		END WHILE;

		SET stacktrace = TRIM(stacktrace);

		SET text_out = tempo.Text;
		IF FIELDNAME(start) = 'Insert'
		THEN
			WHILE LASTMOVE(start)
			DO
				SET text_out = text_out || ' : ' || start.Text;
				MOVE start NEXTSIBLING NAME 'Insert';
			END WHILE;
		END IF;

		SET stacktrace_out= stacktrace;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/apps/finance-accounting-integration-invoices-ingestion/SapResponseMapping.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE SapResponseMapping
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		DECLARE sapResponse REFERENCE TO InputRoot.DataObject.*;
		
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.meta.submissionId = COALESCE(NULLIF(Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id", ''),COALESCE(NULLIF(Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id", ''),COALESCE(NULLIF(Environment.Variables.trackID, ''), UUIDASCHAR)));
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.meta.timestamp = COALESCE(sapResponse.SapTechnicalReponse.SapMeta.TIMESTAMP, CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyy-MM-dd''T''HH:mm:ss'));
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.status = COALESCE(sapResponse.SapTechnicalReponse.SapData.STATUS, '');
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.receivedCount = CAST(sapResponse.SapTechnicalReponse.SapData.RECEIVEDCOUNT AS INTEGER DEFAULT 0);
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.message = COALESCE(sapResponse.SapTechnicalReponse.SapData.MESSAGE, '');

		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers".submissionId = OutputRoot.JSON.Data.meta.submissionId;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers".status = OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.status;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers".receivedCount = OutputRoot.JSON.Data.data.receivedCount;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 202;

		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."INVOICE_ID";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."business-identifiers"."FIP_NUMBER";

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

BROKER SCHEMA sarge

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareHTTPRequestMapping
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = Environment.Variables.Policy.RequestURL_invoice ;
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'POST';
		
        CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data TYPE JSON.Array;
        DECLARE outRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
        
		DECLARE invoiceCount INTEGER CARDINALITY(InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[]);
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		-- Loop through all invoices
		WHILE I <= invoiceCount DO
			
			DECLARE refInvoice REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[I];
			
	        -- Create the I item in the array
	        CREATE FIELD outRef.Item[I];
	        DECLARE refItem REFERENCE TO outRef.Item[I];
	        
	        -- Create the "process-control" object
	        CREATE FIELD refItem."process-control"."specification-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
	        DECLARE refSpecIdentifier REFERENCE TO refItem."process-control"."specification-identifier";
	        SET refSpecIdentifier.Item[1]."content" = 'SARGE_INTERVENTION_CUSTOMER_BILLING';
	        
	        -- Invoice type code and currency
	        IF refInvoice.document_type = 1
	        THEN
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = 380;
	        ELSEIF refInvoice.document_type = 2
	        THEN
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = 381;
	        ELSE
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = refInvoice.document_type;
	        END IF;

	        SET refItem."invoice-currency-code".content = 'CHF';
			SET refItem."precending-invoice-reference".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.invoice_id, '');
	             
	        -- Create the "invoice-number" array
	        CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-number" TYPE JSON.Array;
	        DECLARE refInvNum REFERENCE TO refItem."invoice-number";
	        SET refInvNum.Item[1]."content" = CAST(refInvoice.id AS CHARACTER);
	        SET refInvNum.Item[1]."scheme-identifier" = 'INVOICE_ID';
	        SET refInvNum.Item[2]."content" = CAST(refInvoice.patient.fip_number AS CHARACTER);
	        SET refInvNum.Item[2]."scheme-identifier" = 'PATIENT_FIP_NUMBER';
	
			-- Invoice issue date
	        SET refItem."invoice-issue-date".content = refInvoice.date;
	
	        -- Seller details
	        SET refItem.seller."seller-name".content = 'SARGE';
	        SET refItem.seller."seller-postal-address"."country-code".content = 'CH';
	
	        -- VAT breakdown
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-taxable-amount".content = 0;
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-tax-amount".content = 0;
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-code".content = 'E';
	
			-- Document totals
			CREATE FIELD refItem."document-totals";
			
			-- Amount due for payment
			SET refItem."document-totals"."amount-due-for-payment".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Sum of invoice line net amount
			SET refItem."document-totals"."sum-of-invoice-line-net-amount".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Invoice total amount without VAT
			SET refItem."document-totals"."invoice-total-amount-without-vat".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Invoice total amount with VAT
			SET refItem."document-totals"."invoice-total-amount-with-vat".content = refInvoice.total_price;
	
			-- Create the "invoice-note" array
			CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-note" TYPE JSON.Array;
			DECLARE refInvNote REFERENCE TO refItem."invoice-note";
			CREATE FIELD refInvNote.Item[1];
		
			SET refInvNote.Item[1]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_TYPE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[1]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.type, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[2]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_START_TIME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[2]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.start_time, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[3]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_END_TIME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[3]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.end_time, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[4]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[4]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[5]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_STREET';
			SET refInvNote.Item[5]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_street, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[6]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_STREET_NUMBER';
			SET refInvNote.Item[6]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_street_number, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[7]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_SITE_COMPLEMENT';

			SET refInvNote.Item[46]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.gender_name, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[47]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ALARMED_BY_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[47]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.alarmed_by_id, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[48]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.alarmed_by_name, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[49]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'REFERENCE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[49]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.reference, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[50]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'COMMENTS';
			SET refInvNote.Item[50]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.comments, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[51]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LEGAL_REPRESENTATIVE_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[51]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.legal_representative_id, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[52]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LEGAL_REPRESENTATIVE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[52]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.legal_representative_name, '');

			-- Buyer details
			CREATE FIELD refItem.buyer."buyer-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
			CREATE FIELD refItem.buyer."buyer-identifier".Item[1]."scheme-identifier" VALUE 'SARGE';
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-identifier".Item[1].content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.code, '');

			IF refInvoice.debtor.type_id = 2
			THEN
				SET refItem.buyer."buyer-trading-name".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.name, '');
			END IF;
			
			-- Buyer name (lastname|firstname if both present)
			IF refInvoice.debtor.type_id = 1
			THEN
				SET refItem.buyer."buyer-name".content =
				CASE 
			        WHEN refInvoice.debtor.lastname IS NOT NULL AND refInvoice.debtor.firstname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			        	COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.lastname, '') || '|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.firstname, '')
			        WHEN refInvoice.debtor.lastname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			        	COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.lastname, '') || '|'
			   		WHEN refInvoice.debtor.firstname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			         	'|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.firstname, '')
			    	ELSE ''
			  	END;
			END IF;
			-- Buyer postal address
			-- Address line 1 (street|street_number if both present)
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-1".content =
				CASE 
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street IS NOT NULL AND refInvoice.debtor.street_number IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street, '') || '|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street_number, '')
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street, '')
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street_number IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street_number, '')
			  		ELSE ''
			  	END;
			
			-- Address line 2
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-2".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.complement, '');	
			-- Address line 3
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-3".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.po_box, '');		
			-- Post code
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."post-code".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.postal_code, '');		
			-- City
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address".city.content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.city, '');
	
			-- Country code
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."country-code".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.country_code, '');	
					    
			 -- Create the "invoice-line" array
			CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-line" TYPE JSON.Array;		
			DECLARE articleRef REFERENCE TO refInvoice.articles.Item[1];
			DECLARE i INTEGER 1;
			
			-- Loop through the articles in the input JSON
			WHILE LASTMOVE(articleRef) DO
				SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-description".content = COALESCE(articleRef.line_2, '');
			   
			    -- Add "item-name" object inside "item-information"
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-name".content = COALESCE(articleRef.line_1, '');
			
			    -- Add "item-sellers-identifier" array inside "item-information"
			    CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier".Item[1]."content" = COALESCE(articleRef.code, '');
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier".Item[1]."scheme-identifier" = 'ARTICLE_CODE';
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-quantity" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-quantity".content = COALESCE(CAST(CAST(articleRef.quantity AS DECIMAL) AS CHARACTER FORMAT '##0.00'), '');
			
			    -- Add "price-details" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."price-details"."item-net-price".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.unit_price AS CHARACTER), '');
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-line-net-amount" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-line-net-amount".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.price AS CHARACTER), '');
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-quantity-unit-of-measure-code" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-quantity-unit-of-measure-code".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.unit_name AS CHARACTER), '');
			    
			    MOVE articleRef NEXTSIBLING;
			    SET i = i + 1;
			END WHILE;
			
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

BROKER SCHEMA sarvd


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareHTTPRequestMapping
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = Environment.Variables.Policy.RequestURL_invoice ;
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'POST';
		
        CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data TYPE JSON.Array;
        DECLARE outRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
        
		DECLARE invoiceCount INTEGER CARDINALITY(InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[]);
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		-- Loop through all invoices
		WHILE I <= invoiceCount DO
			
			DECLARE refInvoice REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data.invoices.[I];
			
	        -- Create the I item in the array
	        CREATE FIELD outRef.Item[I];
	        DECLARE refItem REFERENCE TO outRef.Item[I];
	        
	        -- Create the "process-control" object
	        CREATE FIELD refItem."process-control"."specification-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
	        DECLARE refSpecIdentifier REFERENCE TO refItem."process-control"."specification-identifier";
	        SET refSpecIdentifier.Item[1]."content" = 'SARVD_INTERVENTION_CUSTOMER_BILLING';
	        
	        -- Invoice type code and currency
	        IF refInvoice.document_type = 1
	        THEN
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = 380;
	        ELSEIF refInvoice.document_type = 2
	        THEN
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = 381;
	        ELSE
	        	SET refItem."invoice-type-code".content = refInvoice.document_type;
	        END IF;

	        SET refItem."invoice-currency-code".content = 'CHF';
			SET refItem."precending-invoice-reference".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.invoice_id, '');
	             
	        -- Create the "invoice-number" array
	        CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-number" TYPE JSON.Array;
	        DECLARE refInvNum REFERENCE TO refItem."invoice-number";
	        SET refInvNum.Item[1]."content" = CAST(refInvoice.id AS CHARACTER);
	        SET refInvNum.Item[1]."scheme-identifier" = 'INVOICE_ID';
	        SET refInvNum.Item[2]."content" = CAST(refInvoice.patient.fip_number AS CHARACTER);
	        SET refInvNum.Item[2]."scheme-identifier" = 'PATIENT_FIP_NUMBER';
	
			-- Invoice issue date
	        SET refItem."invoice-issue-date".content = refInvoice.date;
	
	        -- Seller details
	        SET refItem.seller."seller-name".content = 'SARVD';
	        SET refItem.seller."seller-postal-address"."country-code".content = 'CH';
	
	        -- VAT breakdown
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-taxable-amount".content = 0;
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-tax-amount".content = 0;
	        SET refItem."vat-breakdown"."vat-category-code".content = 'E';
	
			-- Document totals
			CREATE FIELD refItem."document-totals";
			
			-- Amount due for payment
			SET refItem."document-totals"."amount-due-for-payment".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Sum of invoice line net amount
			SET refItem."document-totals"."sum-of-invoice-line-net-amount".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Invoice total amount without VAT
			SET refItem."document-totals"."invoice-total-amount-without-vat".content = refInvoice.total_price;
			
			-- Invoice total amount with VAT
			SET refItem."document-totals"."invoice-total-amount-with-vat".content = refInvoice.total_price;
	
			-- Create the "invoice-note" array
			CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-note" TYPE JSON.Array;
			DECLARE refInvNote REFERENCE TO refItem."invoice-note";
			CREATE FIELD refInvNote.Item[1];
		
			SET refInvNote.Item[1]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_TYPE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[1]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.type, '');
			
			SET refInvNote.Item[2]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_START_TIME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[2]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.start_time, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[3]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_END_TIME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[3]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.end_time, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[4]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.site_name, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[43]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.type_id, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[44]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.type_name, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[48]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'INTERVENTION_ALARMED_BY_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[48]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.intervention.alarmed_by_name, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[49]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'REFERENCE';
			SET refInvNote.Item[49]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.reference, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[50]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'COMMENTS';
			SET refInvNote.Item[50]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.comments, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[51]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LEGAL_REPRESENTATIVE_ID';
			SET refInvNote.Item[51]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.legal_representative_id, '');

			SET refInvNote.Item[52]."invoice-note-subject-code".content = 'PATIENT_LEGAL_REPRESENTATIVE_NAME';
			SET refInvNote.Item[52]."invoice-note-text".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.patient.legal_representative_name, '');

			-- Buyer details
			IF refInvoice.debtor.type_id = 2
			THEN
				SET refItem.buyer."buyer-trading-name".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.name, '');
			END IF;
			
			-- Buyer name (lastname|firstname if both present)
			IF refInvoice.debtor.type_id = 1
			THEN
				SET refItem.buyer."buyer-name".content =
				CASE 
			        WHEN refInvoice.debtor.lastname IS NOT NULL AND refInvoice.debtor.firstname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			        	COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.lastname, '') || '|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.firstname, '')
			        WHEN refInvoice.debtor.lastname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			        	COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.lastname, '') || '|'
			   		WHEN refInvoice.debtor.firstname IS NOT NULL THEN 
			         	'|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.firstname, '')
			    	ELSE ''
			  	END;
			END IF;
			-- Buyer postal address
			-- Address line 1 (street|street_number if both present)
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-1".content =
				CASE 
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street IS NOT NULL AND refInvoice.debtor.street_number IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street, '') || '|' || COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street_number, '')
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street, '')
			      	WHEN refInvoice.debtor.street_number IS NOT NULL THEN 
			      		COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.street_number, '')
			  		ELSE ''
			  	END;
			
			-- Address line 2
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-2".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.complement, '');	
			-- Address line 3
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."address-line-3".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.po_box, '');		
			-- Post code
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."post-code".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.postal_code, '');		
			-- City
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address".city.content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.city, '');
	
			-- Country code
			SET refItem.buyer."buyer-postal-address"."country-code".content = COALESCE(refInvoice.debtor.country_code, '');	
					    
			 -- Create the "invoice-line" array
			CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-line" TYPE JSON.Array;		
			DECLARE articleRef REFERENCE TO refInvoice.articles.Item[1];
			DECLARE i INTEGER 1;
			
			-- Loop through the articles in the input JSON
			WHILE LASTMOVE(articleRef) DO
				SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-description".content = COALESCE(articleRef.line_2, '');
			   
			    -- Add "item-name" object inside "item-information"
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-name".content = COALESCE(articleRef.line_1, '');
			
			    -- Add "item-sellers-identifier" array inside "item-information"
			    CREATE FIELD refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier" TYPE JSON.Array;
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier".Item[1]."content" = COALESCE(articleRef.code, '');
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."item-information"."item-sellers-identifier".Item[1]."scheme-identifier" = 'ARTICLE_CODE';
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-quantity" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-quantity".content = COALESCE(CAST(CAST(articleRef.quantity AS DECIMAL) AS CHARACTER FORMAT '##0.00'), '');
			
			    -- Add "price-details" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."price-details"."item-net-price".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.unit_price AS CHARACTER), '');
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-line-net-amount" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-line-net-amount".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.price AS CHARACTER), '');
			
			    -- Add "invoiced-quantity-unit-of-measure-code" object
			    SET refItem."invoice-line".Item[i]."invoiced-quantity-unit-of-measure-code".content = COALESCE(CAST(articleRef.unit_name AS CHARACTER), '');
			    
			    MOVE articleRef NEXTSIBLING;
			    SET i = i + 1;
			END WHILE;
			
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;
